Conclusion: PolyUQuest 验证了结构感知图增强检索的有效性，提供可交互演示界面，正准备部署为面向学生的 PolyU 问答服务。

Abstract: Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat web pages as flat text, losing the structural and semantic signals encoded in HTML. We present PolyUQuest, a verifiable, structure-aware web RAG framework built on a heterogeneous graph that unifies hyperlink topology between pages, DOM hierarchy within pages, and entity-relation knowledge across pages. A two-tier router dispatches each query to one of three retrieval modes matched to its structural need, including direct block retrieval, cross-page graph traversal, and multi-hop entity reasoning. Every answer is fully verifiable, as each cited block carries its source page, heading path, and entity links so that users can trace any claim back to its structural evidence. We evaluate on the official websites of the Hong Kong Polytechnic University (PolyU), comprising 4,240 pages, 31,086 DOM blocks, 29,119 entities, and 37,680 relations, together with a multi-type evaluation benchmark. PolyUQuest outperforms existing RAG systems in answer correctness, coverage, and faithfulness, while consuming significantly fewer LLM tokens per query. The demonstration provides an interactive interface for inspecting cited answers, comparing retrieval traces across routing modes, and exploring evidence graph paths. PolyUQuest is being prepared for deployment as a student-facing QA service at PolyU.

</details>


### [71] [Understanding Axes of Difficulty For Long Context Tasks Via PredicateLongBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08284)
*Siddhartha Jain,Ameya Velingker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PredicateLongBench 基准，通过多难度轴系统压测长上下文推理，发现前沿模型在难度提升时表现挣扎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文评估多关注平均情况，许多已饱和或缺乏鲁棒性，缺乏系统方法探究模型随难度轴扩展的表现。

Method: 设计 PredicateLongBench，要求模型在长输入中找出满足给定谓词约束（如字典序）的最长连续子序列；引入多个难度轴；提供合成（随机词串）和真实世界（从自然文档采样）两种生成管道。

Result: 前沿模型在难度轴扩展时表现显著下降，基准有效揭示当前长上下文能力的局限；任务概念简单，无需 LLM 生成或判断。

Conclusion: PredicateLongBench 为长上下文推理提供了可扩展、可控、无需 LLM 评判的压力测试基准，有助于系统性诊断模型局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated rapidly improving long-context capabilities, prompting a wave of benchmarks designed to evaluate them. However, existing long-context evaluations - from Needle-in-a-Haystack (NIAH) tests to more recent multi-hop reasoning and summarization tasks - predominantly measure average-case performance, and many are either saturated or lack robustness. Notably absent is a systematic way to probe how models perform as we scale up the difficulty of tasks along various axes. We address this gap by proposing PredicateLongBench, a benchmark that stress-tests long-context reasoning by asking models to identify the longest contiguous subsequence of words in a long input that satisfies given predicates/constraints (e.g., lexicographic ordering), drawn from a broader predicate class. The central innovation of our benchmark is the identification and systematic exploration of multiple different axes of difficulty which test multiple aspects of long context understanding. We provide two complementary generation pipelines - a fully synthetic setup using random word-like strings, and a real-world setup that samples words from natural documents while preserving their distributional properties. We find that frontier models struggle to perform well as we scale up the difficulty of tasks along our axes, demonstrating the utility of our benchmark in understanding the limitations of current long-context capabilities. Furthermore, the tasks in PredicateLongBench, though challenging, are conceptually simple and do not require LLM-based generations or judges.

</details>


### [72] [Psychological Competence as a Missing Dimension in AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08285)
*Marcos Economides,Paul M. Sacher,Samuel Salzer,Alexis Michelle Abellar,Fendi Tsim,Antoine Ferrère*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“心理胜任力”作为面向人类AI系统评估的新维度，主张从单纯技术性能转向人机交互的心理效应评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估框架聚焦技术性能（准确性、鲁棒性、推理能力、策略合规等），但面向人类的AI系统（顾问、教练、导师、伴侣）直接影响用户认知、情绪解读、信念形成、信任校准和决策制定，现有评估忽视这些心理层面的交互效应。

Method: 基于行为科学和人机交互研究，构建心理胜任力的概念框架及核心领域；定义构念、明确边界；提出基于场景的探针、结构化人工评估、模型辅助评估等评估方法。

Result: 心理胜任力包含框架、语气、感知权威性、响应性、不确定性处理、对话引导等交互属性；现有评估方法仅部分覆盖，缺乏直接评估心理效应；心理胜任力应成为模型提供者、部署方、研究者和监管者的核心考量。

Conclusion: 心理胜任力是评估面向人类AI系统现实世界影响的关键缺失维度，需建立系统性评估框架并纳入主流评估实践。

Abstract: Current AI evaluation frameworks focus primarily on technical performance, including accuracy, robustness, reasoning ability, and policy compliance. These measures remain essential, but they are not sufficient for systems that interact directly with users through natural language. Human-facing AI systems are increasingly used as advisors, coaches, tutors, and companions. In these roles, their responses can shape how users reason, interpret emotions, form beliefs, calibrate trust, and make decisions. The relevant unit of evaluation is therefore not only the model, but the human-AI interaction. This paper introduces psychological competence as a missing dimension in AI evaluation. We define psychological competence as the capacity of a human-facing AI system to support user cognition, emotional interpretation, and behavioral decision-making in ways that are appropriate to the user, context, and purpose of the interaction. This includes interaction properties such as framing, tone, perceived authority, responsiveness, uncertainty handling, and conversational guidance. Existing evaluation approaches capture parts of this problem but rarely assess these psychological effects directly. Drawing on behavioral science and human-AI interaction research, we outline a conceptual framework for psychological competence and its core domains. Rather than proposing a specific benchmark, we define the construct, clarify its boundaries, and describe how it may be assessed through scenario-based probes, structured human evaluation, and model-assisted evaluation methods. We argue that psychological competence should become a core consideration for model providers, deploying organizations, researchers, and regulators concerned with the real-world effects of human-facing AI systems.

</details>


### [73] [INTENT: An LSTM Framework for Vehicle Intention Prediction in Intersection Scenarios with Comprehensive Ablation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08316)
*Logine M. Zaki,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LSTM的INTENT框架，在InD数据集上实现路口车辆意图预测（直行、左转、右转），提前2秒预测，准确率达99.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在复杂交互场景（路口、环岛、紧急停车）中需要像人类一样理解驾驶员意图，以实时采取规避动作，提升安全性并辅助轨迹预测。

Method: 基于LSTM的INTENT框架，利用历史轨迹特征预测车辆在路口的三种意图（直行、左转、右转），提前2秒进行分类。

Result: 在InD数据集上进行充分实验和消融研究，达到99.71%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: INTENT框架能高精度、实时地预测路口车辆意图，为自动驾驶的安全决策和轨迹预测提供可靠支撑。

Abstract: Vehicle intention prediction is a pivotal aspect in the agility and safety of autonomous vehicles in all driving scenarios; if genuine enhancement of autonomous vehicles are required, we need to make them adopt human interpretation of driver's intention especially in cases that require a lot of human interaction as well as complex driving behaviors like the ones at intersections, roundabouts and emergency cases such as sudden stops where vehicle intention prediction helps in taking the correct evasive action within a real time period where every second of action makes an impact and can prevent a catastrophe from taking place. In the worst case, it helps minimize the damage and make safety a priority. Intention prediction can also be used to enhance trajectory prediction (intention conditioned trajectory prediction). In this study, The INTENT framework is proposed using LSTM model to predict the vehicle's intention at intersections 2 seconds ahead of the event occurrence to predict whether the cars in intersections are going straight, turning left, or turning right. Various model experiments and ablation study are thoroughly tested on InD dataset achieving 99.71% accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [Blind-Spots-Bench: Evaluating Blind Spots in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08317)
*Matteo Santelmo,Xiuying Wei,Israa Fakih,Felix Bauer,Juan Garcia Giraldo,Chengkun Li,Etienne Bamas,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 blind-spots-bench 基准，旨在通过人类简单但 AI 难的任务揭示现代 AI 模型的盲点。从 AI 课程学生收集的 235 个样本构建，开发自动评分管道评估多种模型。结果显示闭源前沿模型比开放权重模型领先约 10%，且无单一模型在所有任务类型上占优，部分任务对所有模型仍具挑战，凸显该基准作为诊断压力测试的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准可能低估了现代 AI 系统持续存在的盲点，因为模型在标准基准上表现强，却在人类视为简单的任务（如字符串操作、画五条腿的狗）上失败。需要一种新基准来暴露这些盲点。

Method: 从 AI 课程学生收集原始问题，清洗并标注结构化参考解决方案，构建 235 样本数据集，提出任务分类法。开发自动评分管道评估开放权重和闭源语言、视觉语言、图像生成模型。

Result: 闭源前沿模型在 blind-spots-bench 上比开放权重模型高出约 10%，尽管它们在现有基准上表现相当。细粒度分析显示无单一模型主导所有任务类型，某些任务对所有评估模型仍具挑战。

Conclusion: blind-spots-bench 作为诊断压力测试，能有效识别当前现代模型的具体弱点，凸显其价值。

Abstract: Modern AI models achieve strong performance on many established benchmarks, yet they still fail on tasks that humans find almost trivial, such as manipulating a string or drawing a dog with five legs. These examples suggest that existing benchmarks may under-measure persistent blind spots in current systems. We introduce $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$, a benchmark designed to expose such blind spots through tasks that appear simple for humans but remain challenging for modern AI. We collect raw questions from students in an AI course, clean and annotate them with structured reference solutions, and propose a task taxonomy tailored to the resulting dataset of 235 samples. We further develop an automated grading pipeline to evaluate a wide range of models, including open-weight and closed-source language, vision-language, and image-generation models. Our analysis on $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ reveals that closed-source frontier models can substantially outperform open-weight models with even $\approx10\%$ gap, even when they attain comparable performance on existing benchmarks. A more fine-grained analysis shows that no single model dominates across all task types, and that some tasks remain challenging for all evaluated models. These results highlight the value of $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ as a diagnostic stress test for identifying concrete weaknesses in current modern models.

</details>


### [75] [MobiDiff: Semantic-Aware Multi-Channel Discrete Diffusion for Human Mobility Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08357)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Taichi Liu,Desheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobiDiff是一种端到端离散扩散框架，通过直接去噪多通道语义骨架生成人类移动数据，避免了现有方法中昂贵的插值、潜在轨迹构建和粗到精流程，实现了高保真、隐私保护和高效的合成移动数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界移动数据收集成本高且难以共享（隐私担忧）；现有基于扩散的方法依赖连续或潜在时空轨迹，难以原生建模具有显式区域、活动、时间、间隔结构的离散语义事件。

Method: MobiDiff将每个人类签到事件分解为空间、活动、时间三个通道，采用结构化的事件级、群组级、通道级掩码，联合捕捉轨迹级移动模式和事件内依赖，直接去噪多通道语义骨架，端到端生成移动数据。

Result: 在亚特兰大、波士顿、西雅图三个大规模真实数据集上评估，MobiDiff有效保持轨迹长度和时间间隔分布，在更广泛的移动统计指标上保持竞争力，推理速度比最先进方法（如GeoGen）平均快5.3倍。

Conclusion: 离散扩散为合成移动数据生成提供了一个可解释且高效的框架。

Abstract: Human mobility data are essential for transportation optimization, urban planning, and resource allocation, yet real-world mobility data are costly to collect and difficult to share due to privacy concerns. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown promise in synthesizing realistic mobility patterns, but they typically rely on continuous or latent spatio-temporal traces, limiting their ability to natively model discrete semantic events with explicit region, activity, time, and interval structures. To address this issue, we introduce MobiDiff, an end-to-end discrete diffusion framework that efficiently generates mobility data by directly denoising multi-channel semantic skeletons, avoiding the costly interpolation, latent trace construction, and coarse-to-fine realization pipelines widely used in existing diffusion-based methods. Specifically, MobiDiff decomposes each human check-in event into spatial, activity, and temporal channels, and employs structured event-, group-, and channel-level masking to jointly capture trajectory-level mobility patterns and within-event dependencies. We evaluate generation fidelity, privacy-preserving, and efficiency on three large-scale real-world datasets from Atlanta, Boston, and Seattle. Results show that MobiDiff effectively preserves trajectory length and temporal interval distributions while remaining competitive across broader mobility statistics; it is also much faster than state-of-the-art methods, e.g., 5.3$\times$ faster than GeoGen on average during inference. These findings suggest that discrete diffusion offers an interpretable and efficient framework for synthetic mobility data generation.

</details>


### [76] [FedOPAL: One-Shot Federated Learning via Analytic Visual Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08368)
*Lingyu Qiu,Daniela Annunziata,Stefano Izzo,Fabio Giampaolo,Francesco Piccialli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedOPAL框架通过视觉提示作为特征修正器，在零服务端训练成本下解决联邦学习中的非独立同分布数据问题，达到最先进迭代方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联邦学习中通信带宽瓶颈、现有单轮联邦学习方法的高服务端计算成本和超参数敏感性，以及解析联邦学习在非独立同分布数据下静态特征假设失效导致的特征流形错位问题。

Method: FedOPAL框架，使用视觉提示作为特征修正器，通过局部近端约束主动修正异构数据的特征分布到线性可分空间，满足解析联邦学习的理论假设。

Result: FedOPAL在多个基准测试上显著优于原始解析方法，达到与最先进迭代方法相当的精度，同时保持零服务端训练成本。

Conclusion: 为大模型在边缘端的高效协作提供了新的工程范式。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of basic models in edge intelligence, communication bandwidth has become a core bottleneck restricting the scalability of federated learning. Although one-shot federated learning alleviates this problem by minimizing communication rounds, existing iterative fine-tuning or knowledge distillation methods still face challenges such as high server-side computational costs and hyperparameter sensitivity. Analytical federated learning achieves efficient gradientfree aggregation using least-squares closed-form solutions, but in environments with non-independent and identically distributed data, its static feature assumptions fail, leading to feature manifold misalignment and severely impairing model performance. To address this contradiction, this paper proposes the FedOPAL framework. This framework adapts the visual prompts as feature rectifiers, actively correcting the feature distribution of heterogeneous data to a linearly separable space by applying local proximal constraints, thereby satisfying the theoretical assumptions of analytical federated learning. Experimental results show that FedOPAL not only significantly outperforms the original analytical methods on several benchmarks, but also achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art iterative methods while maintaining zero server-side training costs, providing a new engineering paradigm for efficient collaboration of large models on the edge.

</details>


### [77] [Towards Mechanistically Understanding Why Memorized Knowledge Fails to Generalize in Large Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08393)
*Lu Dai,Ziyang Rao,Yili Wang,Hanqing Wang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Knowing-Using Gap现象，即LLM微调后能记忆新知识但难以用于推理，通过self-patching技术发现知识电路错位，并提出启发式策略恢复58-75%泛化失效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM微调注入新知识时，模型能快速记忆却无法在下游推理任务中使用的关键挑战。

Method: 微调LLM学习未见知识，使用self-patching干预技术监控内部知识空间渗透动态，识别激活位置并重定位表示以改善泛化。

Result: 发现知识电路错位假设成立：记忆表示内部存在但未路由到计算有效层；启发式策略恢复58-75% oracle headroom；跨域实验验证鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM微调知识注入存在Knowing-Using Gap，源于内部知识表示与计算电路错位，通过self-patching诊断并启发式修复可显著缩小泛化鸿沟。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs to inject new knowledge faces a critical challenge: LLMs can quickly memorize new facts, yet fail to use them for downstream reasoning tasks. We formalize this failure as the \textit{\textbf{Knowing--Using Gap}}, characterized by an accuracy gap and a temporal lag between memorization and generalization. To understand this phenomenon, we fine-tune LLMs with unseen knowledge and monitor the spatial permeation dynamics of the knowledge internally using a novel intervention technique called self-patching. Self-patching identifies activation locations where relocating representations substantially improves failed generalization cases. These results are consistent with a knowledge-circuit misalignment hypothesis: memorized representations can exist internally but may not be routed to computation-effective layers. To demonstrate the practicality of this diagnostic finding, we design a simple heuristic strategy which recovers 58--75\% of the oracle headroom in generalization failure. Experiments are done cross-domain for the robustness of this finding.

</details>


### [78] [Game Theory Driven Multi-Agent Framework Mitigates Language Model Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08403)
*Runzhe Liu,Biquan Bie,Zihao Wang,Yuchao Ma,Yexin Liu,Xinghai Li,Harry Yang,Wenbo Yang,Jinzhe Cao,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-Frame，一种融合贝叶斯与团队博弈原理的自适应多智能体框架，通过结构化推理内化领域约束，自动合成高质量推理链与问答对，训练出7B参数的OmniChem模型，在化学基准上达到GPT-4o mini水平，幻觉率降低79.46%，并展示分子设计与合成规划能力，为轻量级LLM在专业科学领域的推理缺陷提供可扩展范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级大语言模型在基于规则的科学领域（如化学）应用受限，主要因其倾向于模仿语言模式而非执行公理推理，导致频繁幻觉，缺乏领域约束的内化机制。

Method: 构建G-Frame自适应多智能体框架，整合贝叶斯推理与团队博弈原则，建立自动化闭环：多智能体协作生成结构化推理链（CoT）与问答对，强制模型内化领域约束，合成专用语料库（363,045条CoT、199,589对QA），基于此训练7B OmniChem模型。

Result: OmniChem在自定义基准与ChemBench上性能持平GPT-4o mini，相对基础架构幻觉减少79.46%；在分子设计与合成规划任务中展现高级能力，验证框架有效性。

Conclusion: G-Frame通过自适应多智能体协作与结构化推理内化约束，实现了高质量数据合成与模型训练的闭环，显著提升轻量级LLM在专业科学领域的推理可靠性，为加速专业知识发现提供可行路径。

Abstract: The application of lightweight Large Language Models in rule-based scientific domains remains severely limited by their tendency to mimic linguistic patterns rather than reproduce axiomatic reasoning, causing frequent hallucinations. Here, we show that G-Frame, an adaptive multi-agent framework integrating Bayesian and team game principles, establishes an automated closed-loop for high-quality data synthesis and model training. By forcing the internalization of domain constraints through structured reasoning, we synthesized a specialized corpus of 363,045 chains-of-thought and 199,589 question-answer pairs. The resulting 7B model OmniChem achieves performance parity with GPT 4o mini on custom benchmarks and ChemBench while exhibiting a 79.46% reduction in hallucinations relative to its base architecture. We further demonstrate the advanced capabilities of OmniChem in molecular design and synthesis planning. This work establishes a scalable paradigm utilizing adaptive multi-agents to overcome inherent reasoning deficiencies, offering a feasible pathway for accelerating knowledge discovery in specialized scientific fields.

</details>


### [79] [OmniFood-Bench: Evaluating VLMs for Nutrient Reasoning and Personalized Health Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08423)
*Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jingpu Yang,Zirui Song,Miao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniFood-Bench 基准，基于 MM-Food-100K 数据集，从基础感知、定量推理、安全关键建议三个层面评估六个最先进 VLMs（如 gpt-5.1、gemini-3-flash、qwen3-vl-8B）。实验揭示“语义-物理鸿沟”：模型在菜品命名上接近人类水平，但在份量估算上灾难性失效，且对高危糖尿病人群频繁产生幻觉性良性建议，确立了公共卫生场景下自主代理可信度的严格标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物基准仅关注粗粒度分类（如食物类别识别），无法评估真实膳食管理所需的复杂推理链——从识别隐性配料到估算物理质量，再到综合安全关键医疗建议。视觉外观与内在营养成分间存在“系统性信息不对称”，亟需更全面的评测体系。

Method: 构建 OmniFood-Bench 基准，包含三个递进能力维度：基础感知（配料与烹饪方式）、定量推理（份量大小与营养画像）、安全关键建议（疾病特定推荐）。在 MM-Food-100K 数据集上评测六个最先进 VLMs。

Result: 模型在菜品命名任务达到近乎人类的准确率，但在质量估算上表现灾难性失效，且针对高风险糖尿病画像频繁产生幻觉性的良性建议，揭示了显著的“语义-物理鸿沟”。

Conclusion: OmniFood-Bench 为部署于公共卫生领域的自主代理建立了严格的可信度评估标准，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into critical infrastructure promises to revolutionize
  personalized healthcare and dietary management. However, in the domain of food systems, autonomous agents face a
  unique and persistent challenge: the "Systemic Information Asymmetry" between visual appearance and intrinsic
  nutritional composition. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on coarse-grained classification tasks, such as food
  category recognition, which fail to evaluate the intricate reasoning chain required for real-world dietary management
  -- specifically, the ability to traverse from identifying hidden ingredients to estimating physical mass, and finally
  synthesizing safety-critical medical advice. In this paper, we introduce OmniFood-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark
  constructed from the MM-Food-100K dataset. Unlike previous works, OmniFood-Bench evaluates VLMs across three
  progressive capabilities: Basic Perception (Ingredients & Cooking Methods), Quantitative Reasoning (Portion Size &
  Nutritional Profiling), and Safety-Critical Advisory (Disease-Specific Recommendations). We evaluate six
  state-of-the-art VLMs, including gpt-5.1, gemini-3-flash, and qwen3-vl-8B. Our extensive experiments reveal a
  startling "Semantic-Physical Gap": while models achieve near-human accuracy in naming dishes, they exhibit
  catastrophic failure in mass estimation and frequently hallucinate benign advice for high-risk diabetic profiles. This
  work establishes a rigorous standard for trustworthiness in autonomous agents deployed for public health. The code
  and datasets are available in: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniFood-Bench-7D0B

</details>


### [80] [Applying JEPA-Style Predictive Learning to JA4-Derived Network Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08465)
*Javier Izquierdo,Aygul Zagidullina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探索将 JEPA 风格的预测式学习应用于网络指纹（JA4），构建了基于 Transformer 的 JA4-JEPA 模型，在 JA4DB 和 CIC-IDS-2017 数据集的约 39.7 万样本上训练。尽管样本间视图重叠不完整，模型在协议族分类（TLS、DNS、SSH）任务上取得余弦相似度 0.9899 和 kNN 准确率 0.9220 的优异结果，证明 JEPA 目标在紧凑网络指纹上同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-JEPA 和 V-JEPA 通过匹配潜在预测与目标编码器输出（而非重建原始输入）在图像和视频领域取得成功。作者旨在验证该相同目标是否适用于紧凑的网络指纹（JA4 指纹）。

Method: 构建 JA4-JEPA：基于 Transformer 的模型，在 JA4、JA4H、JA4S、JA4X 四种子字段上训练。训练数据融合 JA4DB 和 CIC-IDS-2017 约 39.7 万样本，但单个样本不包含全部四类视图。采用 JEPA 预测式学习目标（匹配潜在预测到目标编码器输出）。使用冻结的 kNN 探针在 TLS、DNS、SSH 协议族分类上评估学习到的表示。

Result: 在 39,416 个保留样本上，模型达到余弦相似度 0.9899 和 kNN 分类准确率 0.9220。

Conclusion: JEPA 风格的预测式学习能从 JA4 派生指纹中产生有用的嵌入表示，即使跨数据源的视图重叠不完整也依然有效。

Abstract: I-JEPA and V-JEPA learn by matching latent predictions to target encoder outputs rather than regenerating the original input, and this has worked well for images and video. We explore whether the same objective works for compact network fingerprints. We built JA4-JEPA, a Transformer-based model trained on JA4, JA4H, JA4S, and JA4X subfields drawn from JA4DB and CIC-IDS- 2017. The training data combines roughly 397K samples from both sources, though no single sample contains all four view families. We evaluated the learned representations with a frozen kNN probe on protocol-family classification across TLS, DNS, and SSH. On 39,416 heldout samples the model achieved a cosine similarity of 0.9899 and a kNN accuracy of 0.9220. These results indicate that JEPA-style predictive learning can produce useful embeddings from JA4-derived fingerprints, even with incomplete view overlap across sources.
  Keywords: JA4, network fingerprinting, JEPA, predictive representation learning, self-supervised learning

</details>


### [81] [Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) for Adaptive Semantic Modeling in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08490)
*Bharathwaj Vijayakumar,Sahana K. Varadaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了漂移感知时序图重连（DATGR）框架，通过逻辑更新规则动态调整共现边权重，无需重新训练嵌入即可捕捉生物医学文本中的语义漂移。在BIOMRC语料库上，AUROC绝对提升0.066（0.699 vs 0.633），AUPRC基本持平（0.738 vs 0.744），验证了边级自适应能高效、可解释地建模时序语义演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言随着新发现快速演变，静态嵌入和共现图无法捕捉这种演变，导致检索和知识发现任务性能随时间下降。

Method: 提出漂移感知时序图重连（DATGR）框架，基于估计的语义漂移动态更新共现边权重，采用轻量级的反馈驱动逻辑更新规则，避免了为每个时间片重新训练嵌入的开销。

Result: 在生物医学多关系语料库（BIOMRC）上，AUROC平均绝对提升约0.066（0.699 vs 0.633），AUPRC保持相当（0.738 vs 0.744），表明漂移感知自适应在不损失精度的情况下提升了链接预测召回率。

Conclusion: 边级自适应能有效捕捉不断演变的生物医学文本中的时序语义变化，同时保持计算高效和可解释性。

Abstract: Biomedical language evolves rapidly as new discoveries emerge, causing traditional text models to lose semantic fidelity over time. Static embeddings and co-occurrence graphs cannot capture such evolution, leading to performance degradation in retrieval and knowledge discovery tasks. This paper introduces a Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) framework that models concept evolution by dynamically updating co-occurrence edges based on estimated semantic drift. Instead of retraining embeddings for each time slice, DATGR performs lightweight, feedback-driven rewiring using a logistic update rule applied to edge weights. Evaluated on the Biomedical Multi-Relation Corpus (BIOMRC), the method achieved a mean Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) improvement of approximately 0.066 absolute difference (0.699 vs. 0.633) over a static baseline. Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) remained comparable (0.738 vs. 0.744), showing that drift-aware adaptation enhances link-prediction recall without a loss in precision. These results demonstrate that edge-level adaptation effectively captures temporal semantic change in evolving biomedical text while remaining computationally efficient and interpretable.

</details>


### [82] [AI-guided stimuli discovery and generation to optimize facial emotion perception studies in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08533)
*Kushin Mukherjee,Na Yeon Kim,Maren Wehrheim,Ralph Adolphs,Kohitij Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型引导的框架，通过训练人群特异性神经网络模型预测自闭症与神经典型成人的面部情绪判断，筛选并生成能最大化或最小化群体差异的刺激图像，实验验证了该方法能有效揭示神经多样性感知差异的条件依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中自闭症与神经典型成人在面部情绪感知上的群体差异发现不一致，可能源于刺激图像层面的稀疏性——差异集中在少数诊断性表情而非均匀分布。需要开发敏感、可靠且具机制解释力的行为测定方法，从固定刺激集的平均分析转向优化的、条件依赖的检测范式。

Method: 1. 训练自闭症和神经典型人群特异性的神经网络模型预测单图像层面的情绪判断；2. 利用模型筛选预测能最大化群体分离度的新面孔图像；3. 在独立队列中验证模型筛选图像与随机图像的行为差异；4. 结合生成对抗网络（GAN）将诊断性图像转化为预测群体一致性更高的版本；5. 通过表型匹配验证合成图像相对于原图的行为分离度变化。

Result: 模型筛选的图像在独立队列中产生的行为差异显著大于匹配的随机图像；GAN合成的高一致性图像在表型匹配验证中显著降低了行为分离度。证明了图像层面的稀疏性是导致研究间差异的关键因素，且模型引导的刺激优化能有效放大或缩小群体感知差异。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了模型引导的刺激发现与转化框架，证明行为表型分析可以超越固定刺激集的平均化方法，转向优化检测范式，精准识别神经多样性感知发散或收敛的具体条件，为个体差异研究提供了新的方法学范式。

Abstract: Understanding perceptual differences between autistic and neurotypical adults requires behavioral assays that are sensitive, reliable, and mechanistically informative. Facial emotion perception is a useful test case because group differences have been reported, but findings vary across studies. Here we show that this variability may reflect image-level sparsity: autistic-neurotypical differences in emotion judgments were concentrated in a small subset of diagnostic facial expressions rather than spread uniformly across stimuli. We trained population-specific artificial neural network models to predict image-level judgments for autistic and neurotypical participants, then used these models to select novel faces predicted to maximize group separation. In an independent cohort, model-selected images produced larger behavioral differences than matched random images. We then used the same models with a generative adversarial network to transform diagnostic images toward greater predicted group agreement. In phenotype-matched validation, synthesized images reduced behavioral separation relative to their matched originals. These results establish a model-guided framework for discovering and transforming stimuli that reveal population-specific perceptual differences. More broadly, they show how behavioral phenotyping can move beyond averaging across fixed stimulus sets toward optimized assays that identify the conditions under which neurodivergent perception diverges or converges.

</details>


### [83] [CommuniWave:A Machine Learning Model for Quantifying the Degree of Temporary Informal Behavior in Urban Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08554)
*Hongye Yang,Shien Liu,Zhihao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出CommuniWave模型，利用机器学习从街道视频中检测和量化城市社区的非正式行为程度(DIB)，生成DIB波动图表以支持动态监测和精细化决策，旨在提升社区地域韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社区规划多采用自上而下的方式，缺乏量化居民非正式行为的有效指标，导致规划与实际行为频繁冲突，难以应对复杂性和不确定性带来的挑战。

Method: 构建CommuniWave模型，集成基于mmaction2的行为捕捉网络(BCN)、自研YOLOv10模型(YLX)以及基于随机森林的行为评估模型(BEM)，从街道视频中提取行为特征并计算DIB。

Result: 模型能高效生成DIB波动图表，实现对社区非正式行为的动态监测，为城市管理者提供决策支持，有助于提升社区整体韧性。

Conclusion: CommuniWave通过机器学习量化非正式行为，弥补了传统规划缺乏行为度量的不足，为增强城市社区地域韧性提供了新的技术手段和决策依据。

Abstract: For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.

</details>


### [84] [SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08573)
*Adis Alihodzic,Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视基于XAI的自适应融合（XGAF），一种基于树的单模态与跨模态专家混合模型，其样本级权重源于TreeSHAP归因幅度。重点分析专家特征维度不均时SHAP归因归约（mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs）的影响。实验表明sum-abs归约能保持总归因质量，使XGAF在MELD和CMU-MOSEI上接近或超越早期融合，显著优于晚期融合。增益主要来自跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂的逐样本路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期融合虽准确但整体性强，缺乏模块化；晚期融合模块化但可能丢失跨模态交互。现有XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF）利用TreeSHAP归因幅度加权专家，但专家间特征维度不均时，不同SHAP归因归约方式（mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs）对高维跨模态专家的抑制效应不同，需系统实证分析。

Method: 构建树状混合模型，包含单模态专家（文本、音频、视频）和跨模态专家（双模态、三模态）。使用TreeSHAP计算每个专家对预测的归因幅度，对比mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs三种归约方式得到样本级专家权重。在MELD 7分类情感识别和CMU-MOSEI 3分类情感识别任务上评估，采用三种面部序列聚合器（包括Transformer），并进行McNemar显著性检验及消融实验。

Result: MELD上sum-abs XGAF Transformer变体WF1达0.5983，早期融合0.6018，晚期融合0.4598；McNemar检验显示sum-abs XGAF与早期融合无显著差异（p=1.000），但显著优于晚期融合（p<0.0001）。CMU-MOSEI上sum-abs XGAF WF1达0.6519，略超早期融合0.6485和晚期融合0.5696。消融实验证实主要增益源于增加跨模态专家（特别是三模态专家），而非复杂路由。诊断显示mean-abs和median-abs权重近乎均匀，sum-abs权重集中于三模态专家。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是透明的实证分析，揭示了SHAP归因归约方式、专家特征维度不均以及跨模态专家设计如何共同影响模块化多模态融合性能。sum-abs归约能保持总归因质量，避免高维跨模态专家被抑制，使XGAF在保持模块化的同时达到媲美早期融合的准确度。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCC-STAR 是一个临床对齐的大语言模型，能阅读电子病历叙事，输出基于风险评分的分期、符合指南的排序治疗方案及个性化生存预测。在 6,668 例多中心队列中，其治疗推荐和风险分层性能优于现行指南及 GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro 等模型，依从其建议的中位总生存达 51 个月，显著高于 BCLC (29 个月) 和 CNLC (32 个月)。专家盲评认为其推理可信，模型准确率超越住院医师和主治医师，并能辅助医生更快做出更准确决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 HCC 指南和分期系统提供的类别粗糙，忽视了分期内异质性及电子病历中的临床上下文，缺乏能直接读取非结构化病历并给出可解释、循证、个性化的分期-治疗-预后联合决策支持工具。

Method: 1. 从 SEER 收集约 3 万例 HCC 病例，经临床医生验证的提示词增强流程扩展为电子病历风格叙事训练数据。2. 构建知识对齐推理框架，采用步级可验证的复合奖函数优化，超越文本层面的指南记忆。3. 在中国 12 家医院 6,668 例多中心队列上评估，对比临床指南、GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro 等基线。4. 进行假设性总生存分析及临床医生盲评。

Result: 1. 治疗推荐与风险分层达到 SOTA，优于指南及竞争模型。2. 依从 HCC-STAR 建议的假设中位总生存 51 个月，显著优于 BCLC (29 个月) 和 CNLC (32 个月)。3. 盲评中肝胆胰专家认为其推理和循证依据可信。4. 模型治疗准确率超越住院医师和主治医师；作为助手可使医生更快、更准地决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR 是一个可靠、可验证的决策支持系统，能实现 HCC 的精准分层与个体化治疗，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [86] [The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08625)
*João Matos,Olivia Buege,Donny Cheung,Gary S. Collins,Paula Dhiman,Nan Li,Bingyu Mao,Benjamin W. Nelson,Michail Ouroutzoglou,Paul Varghese,Jonathan Amar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了2,053真实患者-聊天机器人对话，开发了一个分别建模临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器，通过图灵风格评估验证其真实度（人类评分员准确率仅55%），并利用5种患者人设在1,164个临床分级病例上评估4个LLM的紧急程度分诊性能，发现沟通风格会显著改变分诊结果，强调面向患者的对话式AI必须适应沟通多样性，否则在现实部署中会表现不佳并放大健康差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向消费者的健康聊天机器人开发与评估多依赖配合度高、表达清晰的模拟患者，忽视了真实用户沟通模式和情绪表达的巨大差异，导致系统在理想化交互下表现良好但在真实部署时性能下降并可能加剧健康不平等。

Method: 1) 分析2,053真实患者-聊天机器人对话，量化沟通模式与情绪表达的多样性；2) 构建患者模拟器，分离建模临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略、沟通风格四个维度；3) 采用图灵风格评估，邀请15名人类评分员判别模拟对话与真实对话；4) 设计5种患者人设，在1,164个经临床医生分级的病例上评估4个LLM的紧急程度分诊性能。

Result: 1) 真实患者的沟通模式和情绪表达差异巨大；2) 模拟器生成的对话与真实对话几乎无法区分，人类评分员准确率仅55%；3) 沟通风格显著改变分诊结果，同一临床病例在不同人设下会导致不同的紧急程度判定。

Conclusion: 面向患者的对话式人工智能必须适应沟通多样性；仅针对理想化交互设计的系统在现实部署时会表现不佳，并可能放大健康差异。

Abstract: Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [87] [Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08652)
*Eugene Ng Yi Sheng,Bingquan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过多智能体市场模拟研究自利智能体在重复社会困境中维持合作的形式化机制，发现调解机制在对抗攻击下表现出鲁棒性，能维持正向效用且不可被彻底击垮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在无限制通信基础上，何种形式化机制足以让自利智能体社会维持市场稳定，以及这些机制对抗敌对攻击的韧性。

Method: 使用18个DeepSeek-V3 LLM智能体进行多智能体市场模拟，智能体具有互补生产专长，在受限社交网络中交易。两阶段实验：(1) 八种机制对比，渐进式注入搅局者，持续200轮；(2) 对调解机制进行对抗红队测试，使用迭代提示词优化的LLM驱动搅局者。

Result: 调解机制在八种机制中表现最佳；最佳攻击(v6)使诚实智能体效用下降13.3%，但无法导致市场崩溃；调解机制在持续对抗压力下仍能恢复。

Conclusion: 调解机制具有对抗鲁棒性：可被弯曲但不可被打破，能在优化攻击下维持诚实智能体的正向效用。

Abstract: Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.

</details>


### [88] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SolarChain-Eval，一个面向去中心化能源市场的物理约束基准，用于评估经济智能体的可信度，结合 Gymnasium 兼容的 MDP 框架与 LLM 规划/审计层，实验揭示效用-安全权衡，强调物理约束与透明干预记录的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体 AI 系统在网络物理环境中的应用增加，评估需同时考量任务性能与可信度。去中心化能源市场中，自主智能体虽能提升市场效用，但也可能利用无效物理数据、制造人造流动性、导致治理决策不稳定，因此需要一个物理约束的基准来全面评估可信经济智能体。

Method: SolarChain-Eval 将市场治理建模为兼容 Gymnasium 的马尔可夫决策过程，智能体按小时决策。评估维度包括市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑度、空间公平性、可审计性。引入基于 LLM 的规划/审计层：规划器定义回合级动作边界与审计规则，审计器审查并修正高风险动作，所有干预通过结构化日志记录（触发信号、提议动作、修正动作、审计理由）。

Result: 实验对比静态、随机、近视、RL 和 RL+LLM 策略，显示出明显的效用-安全权衡。RL 智能体提升市场效用但仍可能产生不安全行为；移除物理惩罚后，奖励最大化智能体会利用无效发电并增加人造流动性。LLM 规划/审计层改善了可审计性并缓解部分风险，但无法完全弥补奖励函数设计错误。

Conclusion: 可信智能体 AI 评估需要同时具备物理约束和透明的干预追踪。作者已在 GitHub 开源数据与代码以保证可复现性。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


### [89] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种主动记忆干预机制，通过独立的记忆体在长时任务中提取并注入关键状态提醒，显著优于被动检索基线，在两个基准测试上分别提升8.3和6.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时任务中决策相关状态分散在不断增长的轨迹中，随着上下文窗口溢出或被掩埋，导致关键信息无法在需要时影响决策，作者称此为“行为状态衰减”。

Method: 设计一个独立的记忆体与未修改的行动体并行运行：记忆体从近期轨迹更新结构化记忆库，并自主决定是否注入基于记忆的提醒或保持静默。该模块可即插即用地集成到现有前沿行动体与智能体框架中。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上pass@1提升+8.3 pp，在τ²-Bench上提升+6.8 pp，对弱强行动体均有增益。消融实验表明选择性干预优于被动记忆库暴露、持续注入、仅顾问指导和通用检索。进一步在SETA上用SFT和GRPO训练Qwen3.5-27B，验证奖励提升并实现对Terminal-Bench的部分迁移。

Conclusion: 将记忆建模为主动干预而非被动检索，通过选择性注入结构化提醒有效缓解行为状态衰减，且具有良好的即插即用性与泛化性，为开放权重记忆策略提供了早期可行路径。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [90] [The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08734)
*Baha Rababah,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Carson K. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出正确性一致性指标评估量化后大语言模型的行为差异，发现即使任务性能看似保持，量化也会导致行为分歧，且注意力机制中查询和键投影对量化更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练量化评估主要依赖准确率和困惑度，但这些指标无法捕捉量化引起的行为变化，存在“假象等价”问题，需要决策层面的评估指标。

Method: 提出正确性一致性指标，测量基础模型与量化变体在正确预测上的重叠；将量化视为注意力权重上的结构算子，用统计和分布度量量化逐层失真；在多模型、多量化方案（8-bit到2-bit）上实验。

Result: 发现中等量化下即出现行为分歧，即使任务性能似乎保持；低比特宽度存在非线性断点；查询和键投影始终比值和输出投影对量化更敏感。

Conclusion: 传统性能指标掩盖了基础模型与量化模型间的行为差异，揭示了假象等价，呼吁超越传统指标进行行为评估。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.

</details>


### [91] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出受Lisp启发的语言无关概念模型，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照、依赖关系作为持久知识对象，区分derive（确定性计算）与infer（LLM中介判断），实现语义持久化，使工作流可检查、可恢复、可审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流系统虽解决执行问题，但缺乏将工作流本身作为可持久化、可检查、可恢复的知识对象的统一语义模型，需解决LLM应用中工具使用、检索、分支、检查点、人工审批等显式工作流的语义持久化问题。

Method: 采用受Lisp启发的概念模型，引入符号形式、对象身份、活镜像思维作为解释透镜，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照、依赖关系表示为共享知识基质中的持久知识对象，核心语义区分derive（基于可用状态的确定性计算）与infer（声明上下文和执行器控制能力策略下的中介LLM判断）。

Result: 提出语义持久化的初步概念框架，工作流不只是产生知识和留下痕迹，而是本身可作为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象；形式化转换语义留待未来工作。

Conclusion: 该模型为LLM工作流提供了统一的知识对象表示和语义持久化视角，强调derive与infer的区分，为可解释、可审计、可恢复的工作流系统奠定概念基础，但需进一步形式化转换语义。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy.
  The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [92] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUTOPILOT-VQA 是一个面向行车记录仪视频理解的以事故为中心的视觉问答基准，旨在评估模型对安全关键事件的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型、大语言模型和多模态大语言模型在自动驾驶任务中取得进展，但评估其能否可靠地推理安全关键事件仍具挑战性，需要一个标准化基准来评估系统在不同场景下的可靠性。

Method: 构建 AUTOPILOT-VQA 基准，包含围绕真实驾驶事故和近事故设计的结构化问题，覆盖天气光照、交通环境、道路布局、路面状况、标志标识、涉事实体、事故发生、撞击位置、可避免性推理等安全相关类别，要求模型回答关于上下文场景属性和事件级事故细节的落地问题。

Result: 发布了 AUTOPILOT-VQA 数据集，作为 AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 竞赛的一部分，提供标准化基准以评估自动驾驶系统在不同场景下的可靠性，支持开发更可解释、鲁棒和安全感知的视觉语言系统。

Conclusion: AUTOPILOT-VQA 通过要求模型回答基于上下文场景属性和事件级事故细节的问题，超越了目标识别，走向了时序落地的安全感知推理，为真实世界自动驾驶的可解释、鲁棒、安全感知视觉语言系统发展提供支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [93] [Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08748)
*Kristina Schaaff,Quintus Stierstorfer,Valerie Heckel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于77,543名远程学习学生的客观日志数据，对AI学习助手Syntea在高等教育中的使用情况进行了大规模描述性分析，揭示了不同性别、年龄、专业群、学位和学习模式下的使用模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于教育聊天机器人的研究多依赖小样本和自报式问卷数据，缺乏大规模的实际使用行为证据，因此需要开展大规模实证研究以填补这一空白。

Method: 采用大规模描述性分析方法，利用77,543名远程学习学生的客观日志数据，考察性别、年龄组、专业群、学位和学习模式等维度上的使用模式。

Result: Syntea已融入许多学习者的学习常规，但使用情况在不同人口统计学和结构性情境下存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI学习支持的进一步开发提供了实证基础，并为高等教育中教育聊天机器人的使用贡献了大规模分析证据。

Abstract: In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.

</details>


### [94] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench)，一个用于科学谱系推理和基于谱系的创意生成的基准，包含1,961个黄金谱系追踪、1,085个精选Idea Genome对象和920对GenomeDiff记录，跨越10个科学领域。实验表明最强系统在谱系推理上仅达27.3%准确率，结构化谱系上下文会重排系统排名而非统一提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准缺乏对AI系统能否遵循科学思想继承结构（如机制继承、修复局限、重组早期工作）的评估，需要一种能捕捉科学思想演化谱系的评测框架。

Method: 构建IdeaGene框架：将论文/提案表示为最小化、类型化、证据支撑的Idea Genome对象集合；用GenomeDiff对齐记录继承、突变、丢失、外部导入、新颖插入六种演化动态。提供两大评测：IG-Exam（42任务类型，1,029实例）测闭式谱系推理；IG-Arena用谱系条件Population-Evolution Score (PES) 评估生成质量，要求提案能作为连贯后代插入给定谱系种群。

Result: 在14个基于LLM的科学家系统上实验：最强系统在谱系推理任务上仅达27.3%精确准确率；结构化谱系上下文会重排系统排名，而非对所有参与者统一提升，暴露出组合性瓶颈。

Conclusion: IG-Bench填补了科学谱系推理与生成的评测空白，揭示了当前LLM在组合性科学继承推理上的根本局限，为未来研发面向科学演化的AI系统提供了严格基准。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Image classification via a quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07754)
*Kumari Jyoti,Rohith Babu,Apoorva D. Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合经典-量子框架，利用量子启发的专家混合策略进行图像分类，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上表现优于单一专家，错误率降低约一半，且在GPU上开销适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卷积神经网络使用基于扩散的模糊和块状池化进行下采样，作者旨在提出更高效的量子启发策略，结合专家混合以提高特征提取和分类性能。

Method: 混合经典-量子框架：量子部分包括图像振幅编码、局部酉操作卷积、多专家并行处理（不同参数）及量子稳定子码特征提取；经典部分用全连接神经网络联合处理各专家提取的特征进行图像类别预测。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST基准测试中，联合专家分析优于单个专家，图像分类失败率降低约两倍；量子启发策略在GPU工作站上开销适中，是现有经典方案的实用替代方案。

Conclusion: 该混合框架展示了量子启发专家混合在图像分类中的有效性，且量子部分可在量子处理器上执行，为未来量子机器学习提供可行路径。

Abstract: Pattern recognition problems arise in a variety of physical image processing situations, and convolutional neural networks are a popular scheme for the required feature extraction and classification tasks. The classical networks use diffusion-based smearing and block-wise pooling to downsample the image data and capture important structural features. In this work, we propose and demonstrate a more efficient quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts. It is a hybrid classical-quantum framework. The quantum part consists of amplitude encoding of the images, convolution using local unitary operations, multiple experts processing the same image with different parameters, and feature extraction using quantum stabiliser codes. The classical part then jointly processes the features extracted by different experts using a standard fully connected neural network for image class prediction. Using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets as benchmarks, we demonstrate that the joint expert analysis outperforms the individual expert one, as well as reduces the failure rate of image class prediction by around a factor of two. The overhead of our quantum-inspired strategy is only moderate on GPU workstations, which makes our proposal a practical alternative to existing classical schemes. We also point out how the quantum part of our framework can be executed on a quantum processor.

</details>


### [96] [The Importance of Encoder Choice:A Tabular-Image Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07756)
*Ilia Koloiarov,Diego Coello de Portugal Mecke,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次评估了最先进的表格模型作为多模态学习（图像-表格设置）中的编码器，解决了In-Context Learning模型需要标签处理实例的问题，强调了编码器选择在多模态学习中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态学习在处理表格模态时多使用简单的MLP作为编码器，但表格领域仍是深度学习的'最后未攻克的城堡'，说明简单MLP编码器不够强。需要探索最先进的表格模型作为编码器的效果。

Method: 研究评估了最先进的表格模型作为图像-表格多模态学习中的编码器，重点解决了In-Context Learning模型家族需要标签才能处理实例的问题，使得训练和测试实例无法以相同方式嵌入。作者跨多个该家族模型解决了这一问题。

Result: 成功将最先进的表格模型（包括In-Context Learning模型）应用为多模态学习中的编码器，并解决了标签依赖问题，验证了强编码器对多模态学习性能的提升。

Conclusion: 编码器的选择在多模态学习中至关重要，使用最先进的表格模型作为编码器优于简单MLP，且In-Context Learning模型家族的标签依赖问题可以通过适当方法解决，为多模态表格学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal learning usually requires a dedicated encoder per modality. When a tabular modality is involved, prior work has been mostly using a \emph{plain MLP} as the encoder. Yet if it were a strong encoder, the tabular domain would not be ``the last unconquered castle for deep learning''. This study evaluates state-of-the-art tabular models as encoders in the image-tabular setting for the first time. An obstacle stands out. In-Context Learning models, among the best performing methods in the tabular domain, require labels to process instances, making it non-trivial to embed training and test instances the same way. We addressed this problem across multiple models of this family. With this study, we would like to highlight the importance of encoder factor in the multimodal learning.

</details>


### [97] [Scalable and Trustworthy Earth Observation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07758)
*Syed Usama Imtiaz,Mitra Nasr Azadani,Nasrin Alamdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了遥感基础模型（RSFMs）的设计原则，指出地球观测数据的物理约束和操作决策需求要求领域特定适应，基准测试显示无单一模型通用最优且评估不一致是主要问题，提出下一代RSFMs应按基准精度、模态感知迁移和物理合理表示评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域数据量大、重访周期短、多模态，但可靠现场标签稀缺，基础模型范式从孤立任务特定模型转向通用预训练模型，但遥感基础模型不能可靠/最优迁移而无领域特定适应，因为EO数据受测量物理和操作决策约束支配。

Method: 综述章节定义遥感中FMs范式，综合当前模型格局、预训练目标、架构设计、下游适应和可信度要求；结合近期基准证据；提供两个环境监测案例研究：基于物理的光谱靶向掩码用于有害藻华预测，强化学习用于自适应环境监测站选择。

Result: 基准证据表明没有单一地理空间基础模型普遍最优，不一致的评估仍是公平比较和可靠部署的主要问题；案例研究展示了领域指导原则在实践中的应用。

Conclusion: 下一代RSFMs应不仅按基准准确率评估，还应按模态感知迁移和物理合理表示评估，以实现可信的EO决策。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have transformed machine learning from isolated task-specific model development toward general-purpose models pretrained on broad data and adapted to multiple downstream tasks. Earth observation (EO) is an important domain for this paradigm because satellite and airborne archives are large, high-revisit, and increasingly multimodal, while reliable field labels are often sparse. Remote sensing foundation models (RSFMs) cannot be transferred reliably/optimally without domain-specific adaptation. This is because EO data are governed by measurement physics and operational decision constraints. This chapter reviews the design principles arising from these domain-specific constraints. It first defines the FMs paradigm in remote sensing (RS), then synthesizes the current model landscape, pretraining objectives, architecture designs, downstream adaptation and trustworthiness requirements. The chapter also incorporates recent benchmark evidence showing that no single geospatial foundation model is universally best and that inconsistent evaluation remains a major issue to fair comparison and reliable deployment. In addition, two brief environmental monitoring case studies; physics-informed spectral targeted masking for harmful algal bloom prediction and reinforcement learning for adaptive environmental monitoring station selection to illustrate the FMs domain-guided principles in practice. This chapter posits that next-generation RSFMs should be evaluated not only by benchmark accuracy, but also by modality-aware transfer and physically plausible representations for trustworthy EO decisions.

</details>


### [98] [Trustworthy Machine Learning through the Lens of Combinatorial Optimization: Survey and Research Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07762)
*Thibaut Vidal,Julien Ferry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了组合优化（CO）在可信机器学习中的应用，涵盖模型训练、解释、鲁棒性、公平性、压缩、隐私等任务，指出CO方法提供全局保证、形式化证书和显式权衡，尽管可扩展性仍是挑战，但求解器进步预示CO将在可信ML系统设计部署中扮演更重要角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型日益复杂，仅凭经验性能指标难以刻画其行为；性能相似的模型在透明度、可解释性、鲁棒性、公平性、隐私和可认证性等方面差异巨大，需要优化和认证导向的推理框架来支持模型训练、选择、审计和认证。

Method: 综述并综合组合优化与可信ML交叉领域的最新进展，涵盖训练和训练后任务：可解释模型学习、解释生成、鲁棒性分析、公平性审计、模型压缩、隐私攻击与防护；对比CO方法与启发式方法（如梯度法）的优势。

Result: CO形式化提供全局最优保证、形式化证书、显式权衡处理等能力，优于纯启发式方法；可扩展性仍是主要挑战，但求解器和混合算法的持续进步表明CO在可信ML系统设计部署中的作用将日益增长。

Conclusion: 组合优化为可信机器学习提供了强有力的理论框架和实用工具，能够形式化地保证模型的关键属性，随着算法和硬件的进步，其在实际部署中的应用前景广阔。

Abstract: Modern machine learning (ML) increasingly relies on complex models whose behavior is difficult to characterize beyond empirical performance metrics. Across a wide range of tasks, including prediction, generation, and decision-making, models with similar empirical performance can exhibit markedly different properties in terms of their transparency, interpretability, robustness, fairness, privacy, and certifiability. This survey highlights how optimization- and certification-oriented reasoning can provide a useful framework for reasoning about such differences, supporting tasks ranging from model training and selection to auditing and certification. We review and synthesize recent advances at the intersection of combinatorial optimization (CO) and trustworthy ML, covering both training and post-training tasks, including interpretable model learning, explanation generation, robustness analysis, fairness auditing, model compression, and privacy attacks and protections. Across these domains, CO formulations offer additional capabilities over purely heuristic approaches, e.g., gradient-based ones, notably global guarantees, formal certificates, and explicit treatment of trade-offs. While scalability remains an important challenge, continued progress in solvers and hybrid algorithms suggests a growing role for CO in the design and deployment of trustworthy ML systems.

</details>


### [99] [Unlocking Temporal Generalization in Hamiltonian Video Dynamics Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07763)
*Eli Laird,Corey Clark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了 Hamiltonian Generative Networks (HGN) 在非保守系统中的时间泛化失效原因，提出针对性修复，实现了超出训练分布的稳定动力学预测，并为连续时间视频生成的时间泛化提供策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常固定步长，无法在多时间尺度预测；HGN 虽然基于连续时间能量函数，但在外部强迫、耗散环境中时间泛化会失效，限制了分层规划、仿真到现实迁移等应用。

Method: 识别两种失效模式：1) 未约束的动作-力映射导致潜在幅度增长；2) 欠分辨积分器导致全局截断误差累积。针对每种机制提出针对性修复（如约束动作-力映射、改进积分器分辨率）。

Result: 在外部强迫、耗散环境中，修复后的 HGN 能在远超训练步长的时间分辨率下稳定预测动力学，展示了良好的时间泛化能力。

Conclusion: 论文推荐多种策略以实现连续时间视频生成的时间泛化，为分层规划、仿真迁移等应用提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: World models are typically trained to predict discrete-time physical dynamics with a fixed step size baked into the model weights, preventing prediction at variable temporal resolutions. This matters for hierarchical planning, sim-to-real transfer, and scientific or game-engine applications that must query the same dynamics at multiple timescales. Hamiltonian Generative Networks (HGN) offer a principled path forward, grounding predictions in a continuous-time energy function that is, in principle, independent of the observation frame rate. In practice, however, their temporal generalization breaks down in non-conservative settings. We show that in externally forced, dissipative environments, HGN rollouts at step sizes beyond the training regime fail due to distinct failure modes, including latent magnitude growth driven by an unconstrained action-force map, and global truncation error accumulation from an under-resolved integrator. We identify a targeted fix for each mechanism and demonstrate stable dynamics prediction at temporal resolutions well outside the training distribution. In a detailed analysis, we recommend several strategies for enabling temporal generalization in continuous-time video generation.

</details>


### [100] [Principled Analysis of Deep Reinforcement Learning Evaluation and Design Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07769)
*Ezgi Korkmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了强化学习的规范评估与设计范式，引入缩放定律理论基础，通过大规模实验发现算法渐近性能与数据规模间非单调关系，揭示了现有范式下的错误结论，为深度强化学习的规模、容量与复杂性提供了核心分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习过去十年取得显著进展，但缺乏对核心要素、评估范式和设计范式的系统性分析，特别是缩放定律的理论基础及其对算法性能排名的影响尚不明确，亟需深入研究以纠正潜在的错误认知。

Method: 提出强化学习缩放定律的理论框架，分析算法渐近性能与数据规模的关系；开展大规模实证实验，在规范评估与设计范式下对比多种算法在不同数据规模下的表现，验证理论预测。

Result: 发现强化学习算法的渐近性能与数据规模之间不存在单调关系，即性能排名会随数据规模变化而反转；在规范范式下得出的研究结论可能具有误导性，需重新审视算法设计与评估标准。

Conclusion: 强化学习的缩放行为呈现复杂的非单调特性，现有的规范评估与设计范式可能导致错误结论；未来研究应建立更严谨的缩放分析框架，综合考虑模型容量、数据规模与算法复杂性的相互作用。

Abstract: Starting from the utilization of deep neural networks to approximate the state-action value function that led to winning one of the most challenging games, to algorithmic advancements that allowed solving problems without even explicitly stating the rules of the challenge at hand, reinforcement learning research has been the center of remarkable scientific progress for the past decade. In this paper, we focus on the key ingredients of this research progress and we analyze the canonical evaluation and design paradigms in reinforcement learning. We introduce the theoretical foundations of scaling laws in reinforcement learning and show that the asymptotic performance of reinforcement learning algorithms does not have a monotone relationship between performance rankings and data-regimes. We conduct large-scale experiments and our results demonstrate that a line of reinforcement learning research under the canonical design and evaluation paradigms resulted in incorrect conclusions. Our analysis and results provide a core analysis on scaling, capacity and complexity of deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [101] [Graph-Regularized Deep Learning for EEG-Based Emotion Recognition with Psychologically-Grounded Label Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07773)
*Dongyang Kuang,Zizheng Ma,Yushan Zhang,Xiaocong Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图正则化的EEG情绪识别框架，将情绪类别建模为图节点并利用三种互补正则化策略（图标签平滑、基于图拉普拉斯的通勤距离、切片瓦瑟斯坦距离）约束预测符合情绪拓扑，在SEED-IV/SEED-V数据集上跨多种骨干网络实现准确率提升高达5.42%且心理上不合理的误分类减少39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG情绪识别深度学习方法将情绪类别视为孤立标签，忽视了情绪间的心理相互依赖关系，限制了模型性能上限。

Method: 构建图正则化学习框架，将情绪作为图节点，边基于维度情绪理论编码邻近性。引入三种计算复杂度递增的正则化策略：图标签平滑（直观软标签）、基于图拉普拉斯的通勤距离（谱图理论）、切片瓦瑟斯坦距离（图上最优传输），惩罚偏离既定情绪拓扑的预测。在AudioTransformer、Conformer、DCGNN三种代表性骨干架构上验证架构无关性。

Result: 在SEED-IV（4类）和SEED-V（5类）数据集上实验显示：最佳情况准确率提升高达+5.42%，心理上不合理的误分类减少39%。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于提升标准方法所能达到的性能上界，代码将开源。

Abstract: EEG-based emotion recognition is critical for mental health monitoring and affective brain-computer interfaces, yet existing deep learning approaches often treat emotion classes as isolated labels, ignoring their psychological interdependencies. We propose a graph-regularized learning framework that conceptualizes emotions as nodes in a graph where edges encode proximity based on dimensional emotion theories. We adapt three complementary regularization strategies--Graph Label Smoothing (intuitive soft labeling), Commuting distance on graph via Graph Laplacian (spectral graph theory), and Sliced Wasserstein Distance (optimal transport on graph)--ordered by increasing computational complexity. These strategies penalize model predictions that deviate from the established emotion topology. Our framework is evaluated across three representative backbone architectures: AudioTransformer (pure transformer), Conformer (CNN-transformer hybrid), and DCGNN (causal graph neural network), demonstrating architecture-agnostic benefits. Experiments on SEED-IV (4 classes) and SEED-V (5 classes) datasets show consistent improvements: best case up to +5.42% accuracy and 39% reduction in psychologically implausible misclassifications. Ultimately, our framework help raise the upper bound of performance achievable with standard approaches. Code will be released.

</details>


### [102] [A law of robustness for two-layer neural networks with arbitrary weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07778)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了 Bubeck、Li 和 Nagaraj 的猜想：对于通用数据，任意双层神经网络若要拟合带噪标签，其 Lipschitz 常数至少为 √(n/m) 阶（最多一个对数因子），且对权重大小无限制。证明适用于所有连续分段线性激活函数（包括 ReLU），核心是函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖，以及一个 rigidity lemma：在高维球面上，不同超平面上的 kink 无法在通用点相互抵消，从而由 Lipschitz 常数控制每个规范 kink 的系数。二维情况下 rigidity 失效，存在 O(1) Lipschitz 常数的插值构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Bubeck、Li 和 Nagaraj 关于双层神经网络拟合噪声标签时 Lipschitz 常数下界的猜想，先前工作（Bubeck 和 Sellke）仅在参数有多项式界的条件下证明了通用版本，而无界权重的双层情况需要全新论证。

Method: 采用函数空间覆盖替代不可行的参数空间覆盖；核心确定性工具是 rigidity lemma：在 B₂ 和 𝕊^{d-1} (d≥3) 上，实现函数的每个规范 kink 系数受其 Lipschitz 常数控制，因为不同超平面上的 kink 在通用点无法相互抵消。

Result: 对于从 𝕊^{d-1} (d≥3) 或 N(0,I_d/d) 均匀采样的数据，标签在 [-1,1] 且噪声水平 σ²>0，任意宽度为 m 的双层网络（任意实数权重、偏置及仿射跳连），若拟合误差 ε 低于噪声底，则 w.h.p. 有 Lip(f) ≥ c ε √(n / (m̄ log(C m̄ n d / ε)))，其中 m̄ = (K-1)m+1。同一事件上还成立已实现 kink 计数版本：k(f) ≤ n 个不同 kink 超平面的任意双层分段线性函数满足相同界，将 m̄ 替换为 k(f)+1，与冗余隐藏单元数无关。d=2 时 rigidity 失效，显式构造了宽度 2n、Lipschitz 常数 O(1) 的 ReLU 插值网络，达到过参数化端点的界。

Conclusion: 猜想在对数因子内成立，揭示了双层分段线性网络在高维通用数据上的内在 Lipschitz 下界，并指出了二维情况的特殊性。证明技术（函数空间覆盖与 rigidity lemma）为无界权重神经网络的泛化分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Bubeck, Li and Nagaraj conjectured that, for generic data, any two-layer neural network with $m$ neurons that fits $n$ noisy labels must have Lipschitz constant at least of order $\sqrt{n/m}$, with no restriction on the size of the weights. Bubeck and Sellke proved a universal version of this law for Lipschitz-parameterized classes, but under a polynomial bound on the parameters; at depth three that boundedness hypothesis is genuinely necessary. The two-layer unbounded-weight case requires a different argument. We prove the conjectured law, up to one logarithmic factor, for every continuous piecewise-linear activation, in particular for ReLU networks. For data drawn uniformly from $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$, $d\ge3$, or from $N(0,I_d/d)$, labels in $[-1,1]$ with noise level $σ^2>0$, and any width-$m$ two-layer network with arbitrary real weights, biases and affine skip connection, fitting the data $\varepsilon$ below the noise floor forces $\mathrm{Lip}(f)\ge c\,\varepsilon\sqrt{n/(\bar m\log(C\bar m nd/\varepsilon))}$, $\bar m=(K-1)m+1$, with high probability. A realized-kink-count version holds on the same event: every realized two-layer piecewise-linear function with $k(f)\le n$ distinct kink hyperplanes obeys the bound with $\bar m$ replaced by $k(f)+1$, irrespective of how many redundant hidden units parameterize it. The proof replaces parameter-space covering, impossible for unbounded weights, by a function-space covering. The central deterministic ingredient is a rigidity lemma: on $B_2$, and on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ for $d\ge3$, the coefficient of each canonical kink is controlled by the Lipschitz constant of the realized function, because kinks on distinct hyperplanes cannot cancel at generic points. Rigidity genuinely fails at $d=2$, and an explicit two-layer ReLU interpolant with $O(1)$ Lipschitz constant at width $2n$ matches the law at the overparameterized endpoint.

</details>


### [103] [Predicting Pseudo-nitzschia harmful algal blooms along the Portuguese Coast using satellite-derived predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07834)
*Ayman Bnoussaad,El Khalil Cherif,Ligia Pinto,Ramiro Neves,Alexandra D. Silva,Alexandre Bernardino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种基于卫星数据的时空机器学习框架，用于预测葡萄牙大西洋沿岸Pseudo-nitzschia硅藻引起的有害藻华（HAB），集成树模型达到0.74-0.77的AUC，证明了卫星支持的HAB早期预警系统的操作可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决葡萄牙大西洋沿岸Pseudo-nitzschia硅藻反复带来的生态风险和贝类采收威胁，利用卫星衍生预测因子在真实预报约束下预测HAB发生。

Method: 使用2013-2023十年数据（1440观测，1000+卫星预测因子），采用河流感知空间聚类划分采样区域，实施严格的时空交叉验证（同时保留整年和空间簇），训练集成树模型（随机森林、极端树）预测HAB发生。

Result: HAB发生在各模型类和特征配置下均表现出中等可预测性；随机森林仅用环境预测因子达AUC 0.74±0.05，极端树加入生物变量达0.77±0.06；特征重要性显示季节结构、空间背景和滞后环境条件主导模型决策，生物指标在物理有利时期细化 bloom 概率。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了对东部边界上升流海岸卫星支持的HAB早期预警系统具有操作相关技能，可为沿海生态系统管理和贝类产业提供决策支持。

Abstract: Pseudo-nitzschia diatoms pose recurrent risks to coastal ecosystems and shellfish harvesting along the Portuguese Atlantic coast. Here we develop and evaluate a spatio-temporal machine-learning framework to predict harmful algal bloom (HAB) occurrence using exclusively satellite-derived predictors under realistic forecasting constraints. We characterised environmental and biological variability across shellfish production zones (L1-L9) using 5,882 observations, providing system-wide context. Predictive models were developed for zones L1-L2, a hotspot for Pseudo-nitzschia and domoic acid events, using a decade-long dataset (2013-2023; 1,440 observations; more than 1,000 satellite-based predictors including sea surface temperature, an upwelling index, chlorophyll-a, and plankton functional types). Sampling locations were partitioned into ecologically meaningful sub-regions using a river-aware spatial clustering scheme. A stringent spatio-temporal cross-validation strategy that simultaneously withholds entire years and spatial clusters prevents leakage and closely mimics real-world forecasting conditions. HAB occurrence proved moderately predictable across model classes and feature configurations. Ensemble tree-based methods achieved the strongest discrimination: Random Forest reached 0.74 +/- 0.05 with environmental predictors; Extra Trees reached 0.77 +/- 0.06 with biological variables added. Feature-importance analyses revealed that seasonal structure, spatial context, and lagged environmental conditions dominate model decisions, while biological indicators refine bloom likelihood within physically favourable periods. The framework demonstrates operationally relevant skill for satellite-supported HAB early-warning systems along eastern boundary upwelling coasts.

</details>


### [104] [Explaining Near-Zero Hessian Eigenvalues Through Approximate Symmetries in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07845)
*Marcel Kühn,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将损失函数Hessian矩阵的近零特征值本体归因于网络参数化连续对称性被弱打破产生的伪Goldstone模式，通过线性网络精确对称性分析、ReLU非线性微扰理论及实验验证，揭示了高曲率方向正交于对称子空间而本体特征值几乎完全位于其中的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已解释Hessian最大特征值的来源，但大量极小特征值的起源仍不清楚。论文旨在阐明Hessian本体中海量近零模式的根源。

Method: 1. 在深度线性网络中构建精确连续对称性产生的零模式本征向量；2. 引入ReLU非线性作为微扰，分析其如何弱显式打破对称性；3. 通过本征向量层面解析谱结构，区分高曲率方向与对称子空间；4. 在双层ReLU学生-教师模型、CIFAR-10训练网络及卷积网络上实验验证。

Result: Hessian本体由弱抬升的伪Goldstone模式组成，对应网络参数化连续对称性被弱打破。高曲率方向正交于对称子空间，而本体特征值几乎完全位于对称子空间内。该机制在学生-教师模型、CIFAR-10及卷积网络中均得到验证。

Conclusion: Hessian本体特征值源于网络参数化连续对称性的弱显式破缺，将近零模式的起源与弱打破对称性联系起来，为损失地形局部几何提供了统一解释。

Abstract: The Hessian of the training loss governs the local geometry of the loss landscape, yet despite existing explanations for its largest eigenvalues, the origin of the vast multitude of vanishingly small eigenvalues remains elusive. We argue that the bulk consists of the weakly lifted pseudo-Goldstone modes of the continuous symmetries of the network parametrization. In deep linear networks these symmetries are exact: they generate flat directions and hence exact zero modes, whose eigenvectors we construct explicitly. Introducing a ReLU nonlinearity as a perturbation, we show that it breaks these symmetries weakly and explicitly. Resolving the spectrum at the level of eigenvectors, we find that the high-curvature directions are orthogonal to the symmetry subspace, while the bulk lies almost entirely within it. We demonstrate the mechanism in a two-layer ReLU student--teacher model and in a network trained on CIFAR-10. A convolutional example demonstrates that the same diagnostic extends beyond fully connected layers. Together, these results link the Hessian bulk to weakly broken symmetries and clarify the origin of near-zero modes.

</details>


### [105] [When Does Continual Learning Require Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07847)
*Anne Harrington,Nayan Saxena,Michael Murphy,Anastasia Borovykh,Zeyu Yun,Sridhar Kamath,Ara Eindra Kyi,Trevor Darrell,Jitendra Malik,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新定义了大语言模型的持续学习问题，将其拆解为空间（新领域）和时间（数据漂移）两个维度，并通过统一协议评估了提示词方法、监督学习、强化学习和上下文压缩等多种更新策略。结果表明：提示词方法适应快但长期退化；蒸馏方法稳定积累但难以更新过时知识；上下文压缩提升效率但不增强新任务学习能力；在线强化学习最擅长知识更新但对噪声奖励敏感。结论指出持续学习非单一能力，不同环境变化模式需要根本不同的更新行为，需根据变化类型决定将适应学习进模型权重还是通过外部脚手架实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型持续学习研究主要聚焦于上下文管理和缓解灾难性遗忘，作者认为这种框架不完整。持续学习本质上是随着世界变化提升模型能力。作者将环境变化拆解为两个轴：空间维度（模型遇到新领域）和时间维度（固定任务下底层数据漂移）。这种框架允许在真实条件下研究持续学习：新领域随时间到来、事实在训练截止后漂移、智能体交互跨回合累积状态。

Method: 将广泛使用的大语言模型基准重构为序列化问题，引入单一机制无关协议，对比提示词方法（GEPA, ACE）、监督学习（SFT, SDFT）、强化学习（GRPO, SDPO）和上下文压缩（Cartridges, In-place TTT）等四类方法。

Result: 提示词方法能快速适应每个新阶段但在未来任务上性能下降；蒸馏类方法稳定积累知识但难以更新过时事实；上下文压缩提升效率但不显著增强学习新任务能力；在线强化学习对知识更新适应最有效但对噪声奖励信号敏感。

Conclusion: 持续学习不是单一能力：不同环境变化模式需要根本不同的更新行为，这决定了何时必须将适应学习进模型权重，何时可通过外部脚手架实现。了解各方法的优劣将指导更强持续学习系统的设计。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly capable, the next question is how can we enable models to continually learn? Today, the field largely frames this as a problem of context management and mitigating forgetting. We argue this framing is incomplete: continual learning is fundamentally about increasing model competence as the world changes. We disentangle this change along two axes -- space, where the model encounters new domains, and time, where the underlying data drifts under a fixed task. This framing lets us study continual learning under realistic conditions: new domains arrive over time, facts drift past their training cutoff, and agentic interactions accumulate state across episodes. To evaluate methods under this setting, we recast widely used LLM benchmarks as sequential problems and introduce a single mechanism-agnostic protocol that compares prompt-based methods (GEPA, ACE), supervised learning (SFT, SDFT), reinforcement learning (GRPO, SDPO), and context compression (Cartridges, In-place TTT). Prompt-based methods fit each new stage quickly but degrade on future tasks. Distillation-based methods accumulate knowledge stably but struggle to update outdated facts. Context compression improves efficiency without substantially improving the ability to learn new tasks. Online reinforcement learning adapts most effectively to knowledge updates but remains sensitive to noisy reward signals. Overall, our results suggest that continual learning is not a single capability: different patterns of environmental change require fundamentally different update behaviors, determining when adaptation must be learned inside model weights and when it can be achieved through external scaffolding. We hope that understanding where each method succeeds and fails will guide the design of stronger continual learning systems.

</details>


### [106] [NFTR: From Provable Mode-Averaging to Geodesic Subgoal Selection in Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07855)
*Erdemt Bao,Xing Lei,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG
